Task S004 / v0.9.35

Christian Verrecchia · 24 November2026 · internally tested development preview; independent physical/commercial qualification pending.

1. Objective, physical model, equations, SI units, sources, boundaries, numerical algorithm and limitations

# Fitted-feature interval refinement — S004 / v0.9.35

In **Inductance → Mesh refinement**, choose **Every fitted interval** (the new UI default). `Mesh factors: 1,2` halves every base interval, including core, gap and winding intervals. `1,2,4` adds another halving if it fits the resource budget. **Requested global target** retains the preceding S003 behavior and uses factors1,2,3; geometry-dominated target meshes may be identical. Air factors remain increasing unique integers1..3 starting at1. At most6crossed cases are accepted. Every point still freshly solves the full original linear reference matrix and the explicit selected simultaneous-short measurement. The preceding source/project/field are retained.

Source CLI: `python tools/study_ports.py source.json study.json --mesh-mode interval --input-id primary --short secondary --mesh-factors 1,2 --air-factors 1,2`. Use actual canonical matrix IDs. CLI default is interval; pass `--mesh-mode target` to reproduce old target-mode CLI requests. Existing API requests without `mesh_mode` retain target behavior. UTF-8 file read/write is now explicit; actual CLI execution in this increment is Linux, not Windows source-CLI qualification.

For fixed air factor a, construct the original fitted Cartesian base grids using original nx*a,ny*a,(nz*a), with all physical boxes rigidly translated by half the added domain size. For each adjacent base pair [x_i,x_(i+1)], interval mode inserts x_i+(x_(i+1)-x_i)*j/h for j=0..h-1, then retains the final endpoint. h is1,2or4. Every original grid node remains exact. The original two-triangle/six-Freudenthal-tetrahedron split and original last-region-wins assignment are reused. Each refinement multiplies simplex counts by h^d and reduces every base interval by h. New fine triangles/tetrahedra are contained in their original simplices. The tests check this independently by all-vertex barycentric containment, and integrate every material/air tag's measure before/after refinement. It is uniform nested refinement of a fitted mesh, not local error-driven adaptation, smoothing, aspect-quality optimization, higher-order elements or a calibrated estimator. Aspect ratios remain unchanged.

Ordinary solves still use interval_factor=1 and the same geometry/mesh/assembly path. Original planar equations are -div(nu grad Az)=Jz, B=(dAz/dy,-dAz/dx), P1 triangles; spatial equations are curl(nu curl A)=J, B=curl A, lowest-order Nedelec edges and the original gauge treatment. nu[m/H], A[Wb/m], B[T], H[A/m], J[A/m²], energy W[J]=integral(B·H)/2, inductance L[H], linkage lambda[Wb turn]. Exterior boundary is Az=0 in2D, tangential A=0 in3D. The2D paired sources remain Jz=±NI/area, constant extrusion depth; the3D sources remain ideal closed rectangular sheets with Tz=NI/window-height and J=curl T. No finite transformer copper/returns/terminals, nonlinear3D or new AC formulation is added. Only validated linear rectangular projects are accepted; existing declared/assigned AC-solid and B-H/polygon rejection remains.

Each solved matrix obeys K a_j=b_j, L_ij=b_i^T a_j with original canonical same-current grouping. The selected input current is1A, unselected open currents are0, and selected shorts obey lambda_S=0: L_SS i_S=-L_Sp. The reported input is Lpp-LpS LSS^-1 LSp. This is an ideal linear lossless short condition, not a loaded switching/resonant network, finite-frequency voltage/current solution or unique magnetising/leakage T-network. Stats describe original prescribed currents; the short-current field is independently re-solved in tests but not substituted for the retained display field.

Each row exports exact project, mesh_mode, mesh_factor, air_factor, actual cell count and full coordinate/connectivity/material SHA256. Interval factors do not change project nx: **project JSON alone does not reproduce a refined row**; use its mode/factor as well. The execution reconstructs and checks the complete planned mesh hash before assembly. Cells/nodes are predicted before finer-coordinate allocation and checked against original dimensional budgets. Shared study ceilings remain24000actual cells/case and240000cell-excitations total (original-current solve plus every physical unit source). Work grows quickly in3D; a rejected fourth-level mesh is not a convergence result. Desktop preflight remains synchronous before job acceptance; accepted numerical work and mesh reconstruction honour original cancellation. Browser worker termination preserves accepted fields.

Air expansion is still a separate empirical axis. It holds nominal physical spacing and component-relative interior subdivisions at fixed h but changes exterior topology. Empirical relative changes are abs(Lnew-Lold)/max(abs(Lnew),abs(Lold),1e-30), not a boundary-error bound. Identical target meshes remain flagged and excluded from refinement evidence. No automatic converged/pass label is introduced.

The planar fixture is a120×100mm exterior with30mm fixed extrusion, linear relative permeability2000core and40/20/13turn windings; prescribed currents are3/0/0A. The spatial fixture is a120×100×120mm air-domain model with three ideal rectangular sheets of60/17/18turns at different axial supports, prescribed1/0/0A. It has no solved ferromagnetic core or finite copper. Complete exact region/source coordinates are retained in each study JSON and the report. These are bounded numerical fixtures, not full CLLC operating designs.

Actual new evidence is sample_results/interval_refinement/{2d,3d,numerical_summary}.json: the same preceding coarse multiwinding physical fixtures at base targets2. Planar air1 uses286→1144→4576triangles, Lshort119.418337→134.337963→138.564143µH; the last normalized change is3.050%. Spatial air2 uses1350→10800tetrahedra,67.703977→127.613812µH, a46.946%change; at fine h2 air expansion changes128.143505→127.613812µH (0.413%). The coarse3D model remains strongly mesh-sensitive and unqualified. A lower air sensitivity does not remove that mesh error. No Richardson/extrapolated bound, design qualification or commercial/hardware correlation is inferred.

Reproduce from the source root: `python -m unittest discover -s tests -p test_interval_refinement.py -v`; `python verification/run_s004_cases.py`; `python verification/check_s004_defaults.py /path/to/extracted/v0.9.34/magnetics_studio`. The last comparison executes both real2D/3D ordinary field solves in independent interpreters and compares all deterministic result bytes, excluding only stats.seconds and stats.solver.seconds. An initial comparison incorrectly omitted the nested solver timing exclusion and failed on timing only; its actual failure is retained. A first focused invocation lacked PYTHONPATH and failed import before execution; the corrected invocation and final audited profile are credited.

QA/report generators, prior-source snapshots, test DOM/WebAssembly/browser libraries and logs are development-only; no new runtime dependency is introduced. Full independent source/p/order/domain/local-field accuracy, matched adviser/FEM conversion, actual joined finite winding/return/core, native nonlinear/AC, full CLLC, genuine Maxwell/hardware references and final clean permitted Windows10/11 offline/no-Python-installed qualification remain pending. Development previews are authorised; CI smoke does not establish those final qualifications.

Three new methods check every fine simplex against independently computed coarse barycentric containment, exact base-plane retention, per-tag integrated area/volume, unchanged aspect ratio, h^dimension cell growth, full mesh-hash reproduction and actual independently re-solved short-current energy/linkage. They also exercise invalid modes/factors/resource rejection, accepted job/field retention and real interval-mode cancellation. Focused and pure-Python counts overlap full discovery. The complete105existing JavaScript checks and genuine DOM/WebAssembly studies pass. Native Chromium selects interval mode and downloads real newly solved data; Windows CI executes the same actual interval study from the exact frozen executable plus original native-adviser/cancel/export checks.

Ordinary2D/3D fields, complete meshes/matrices and every deterministic diagnostic match the preceding v0.9.34 checkout byte-for-byte in independent interpreters. Both actual elapsed timing fields are excluded explicitly. The initial comparison excluded only outer timing and therefore failed on stats.solver.seconds; the retained corrected comparison fixes evidence methodology, not physical code. An initial focused invocation lacked the source-root PYTHONPATH and failed import before execution; its raw failure is retained and not credited. Only the complete final-source independently audited profile is counted. Windows Server-runner smoke with Python absent from childPATH and proxy restrictions is not clean permitted Windows10/11 offline/noPython-installed qualification.

engine/mesh.py

39def build_mesh(project, progress=None, cancel=None, *, interval_factor=1): #: Keep ordinary meshes unchanged and allow explicit bounded subdivision of every fitted base interval.Keep ordinary meshes unchanged and allow explicit bounded subdivision of every fitted base interval.

42 if interval_factor!=1:raise ValueError('Interval refinement requires rectangular reference geometry') #: Do not silently apply Cartesian refinement semantics to the separate polygon mesher.Do not silently apply Cartesian refinement semantics to the separate polygon mesher.
54 count = math.prod((len(g)-1)*interval_factor+1 for g in grids) #: Predict refined node allocation before constructing new coordinates or elements.Predict refined node allocation before constructing new coordinates or elements.
55 cells = math.prod((len(g)-1)*interval_factor for g in grids) #: Account for every refined physical and exterior-air interval before the original memory guard.Account for every refined physical and exterior-air interval before the original memory guard.

Complete numbered source
0001  """Interface-fitted Cartesian triangle/tetrahedron meshes for boxes.
0002  
0003      Every geometry and winding coordinate is a mesh plane. Each 3D hexahedron
0004      is split into six Freudenthal tetrahedra with globally matching faces.
0005  """
0006  import itertools
0007  import math
0008  from .sparse import check_cancel
0009  
0010  
0011  def dot(a, b):
0012      return sum(x*y for x, y in zip(a, b))
0013  
0014  
0015  def sub(a, b):
0016      return tuple(x-y for x, y in zip(a, b))
0017  
0018  
0019  def cross(a, b):
0020      return (a[1]*b[2]-a[2]*b[1], a[2]*b[0]-a[0]*b[2], a[0]*b[1]-a[1]*b[0])
0021  
0022  
0023  def inside(box, point):
0024      return all(box[axis+'0']-1e-13 <= point[i] <= box[axis+'1']+1e-13
0025                 for i, axis in enumerate('xyz'[:len(point)]))
0026  
0027  
0028  def axis_grid(size, target, cuts):
0029      pts = sorted(set([0., size] + [float(x) for x in cuts]))
0030      result = [0.]
0031      for a, b in zip(pts, pts[1:]):
0032          if b-a < size*1e-8:
0033              raise ValueError('Two geometry planes are too close; simplify the geometry')
0034          n = max(1, math.ceil((b-a)/size*target - 1e-10))
0035          result.extend(a + (b-a)*i/n for i in range(1, n+1))
0036      return result
0037

0039  def build_mesh(project, progress=None, cancel=None, *, interval_factor=1):  #: Keep ordinary meshes unchanged and allow explicit bounded subdivision of every fitted base interval.
0040      if type(interval_factor) is not int or interval_factor not in (1,2,4):raise ValueError('Interval factor must be1,2or4')  #: Require dyadic factors for genuine nested reference study levels; reject booleans and rounded values.
0041      if project['mode']=='2d' and any(r.get('polygon') for r in project.get('regions', [])):
0042          if interval_factor!=1:raise ValueError('Interval refinement requires rectangular reference geometry')  #: Do not silently apply Cartesian refinement semantics to the separate polygon mesher.
0043          from .polygon_mesh import build_polygon_mesh
0044          return build_polygon_mesh(project,progress,cancel)
0045      dim = 3 if project['mode'] == '3d' else 2
0046      domain = project['domain']
0047      objects = list(project.get('regions', []))
0048      if dim == 3:
0049          objects += [w['window'] for w in project.get('windings', [])]
0050      grids = []
0051      for axis, size_key in zip('xyz'[:dim], ['width', 'height', 'depth']):
0052          cuts = [o[axis+end] for o in objects for end in ('0', '1')]
0053          grids.append(axis_grid(domain[size_key], domain['n'+axis], cuts))
0054      count = math.prod((len(g)-1)*interval_factor+1 for g in grids)  #: Predict refined node allocation before constructing new coordinates or elements.
0055      cells = math.prod((len(g)-1)*interval_factor for g in grids)  #: Account for every refined physical and exterior-air interval before the original memory guard.
0056      max_nodes, max_elements = (24000, 46000) if dim == 2 else (24000, 120000) if domain.get('budget')=='extended' else (10000, 44000)
0057      if count > max_nodes or cells*(2 if dim == 2 else 6) > max_elements:
0058          raise ValueError('Mesh exceeds the selected memory budget; lower subdivisions/simplify sections, or explicitly select the extended 3D budget in Builder → Dimensions')
0059      if interval_factor!=1:  #: Retain the exact ordinary mesh path when refinement is not explicitly requested.
0060          check_cancel(cancel)  #: Allow study cancellation before allocating the bounded finer Cartesian grids.
0061          grids=[[a+(b-a)*i/interval_factor for a,b in zip(g,g[1:]) for i in range(interval_factor)]+[g[-1]] for g in grids]  #: Preserve every fitted plane exactly and halve or quarter all base intervals without changing geometry.
0062      if dim == 2:
0063          xs, ys = grids
0064          nodes = [(x, y) for y in ys for x in xs]
0065          nx = len(xs)
0066          elements = []
0067          for j in range(len(ys)-1):
0068              for i in range(nx-1):
0069                  a = j*nx+i; b = a+1; c = a+nx; d = c+1
0070                  elements.extend([(a,b,d),(a,d,c)])
0071      else:
0072          xs, ys, zs = grids
0073          nodes = [(x,y,z) for z in zs for y in ys for x in xs]
0074          nx, ny = len(xs), len(ys)
0075          def index(p):
0076              return p[0] + nx*(p[1]+ny*p[2])
0077          elements = []
0078          for k in range(len(zs)-1):
0079              check_cancel(cancel)
0080              for j in range(ny-1):
0081                  for i in range(nx-1):
0082                      for perm in itertools.permutations(range(3)):
0083                          p = [i,j,k]; tet = [index(p)]
0084                          for axis in perm:
0085                              p[axis] += 1
0086                              tet.append(index(p))
0087                          elements.append(tuple(tet))
0088      tags = []
0089      for e in elements:
0090          c = tuple(sum(nodes[n][i] for n in e)/len(e) for i in range(dim))
0091          tag = -1
0092          for i, reg in enumerate(project.get('regions', [])):
0093              if inside(reg, c):
0094                  tag = i
0095          tags.append(tag)
0096      boundary = set()
0097      sizes = [domain[k] for k in ['width','height','depth'][:dim]]
0098      for n, p in enumerate(nodes):
0099          if any(abs(x) < 1e-13 or abs(x-size) < 1e-13 for x, size in zip(p, sizes)):
0100              boundary.add(n)
0101      if progress:
0102          progress(f'Mesh: {len(nodes)} nodes, {len(elements)} '+('triangles' if dim == 2 else 'tetrahedra'))
0103      steps = [[b-a for a,b in zip(g,g[1:])] for g in grids]
0104      aspect = max(max(a)/min(b) for i,a in enumerate(steps) for j,b in enumerate(steps) if i != j)
0105      quality = {'smallest_axis_interval_m': min(min(g) for g in steps),
0106                 'largest_cell_axis_aspect_ratio': aspect}
0107      if dim == 2:
0108          quality['minimum_triangle_angle_deg'] = math.degrees(math.atan(1/aspect))
0109      return {'dimension': dim, 'nodes': nodes, 'elements': elements,
0110              'tags': tags, 'boundary': boundary, 'grids': grids,'quality':quality}
0111  
0112  
0113  def triangle_geometry(points):
0114      (x0,y0),(x1,y1),(x2,y2) = points
0115      det = (x1-x0)*(y2-y0)-(x2-x0)*(y1-y0)
0116      if det <= 0:
0117          raise ValueError('Invalid triangle')
0118      grads = [((y1-y2)/det,(x2-x1)/det),
0119               ((y2-y0)/det,(x0-x2)/det),
0120               ((y0-y1)/det,(x1-x0)/det)]
0121      return det/2, grads
0122  
0123  
0124  def tetra_geometry(points):
0125      p0,p1,p2,p3 = points
0126      u,v,w = sub(p1,p0),sub(p2,p0),sub(p3,p0)
0127      det = dot(u,cross(v,w))
0128      if abs(det) < 1e-30:
0129          raise ValueError('Degenerate tetrahedron')
0130      g1 = tuple(x/det for x in cross(v,w))
0131      g2 = tuple(x/det for x in cross(w,u))
0132      g3 = tuple(x/det for x in cross(u,v))
0133      g0 = tuple(-g1[i]-g2[i]-g3[i] for i in range(3))
0134      return abs(det)/6, [g0,g1,g2,g3]

0044  def port_study(project, options, progress=None, cancel=None):  #: Run actual independent unit-excitation solves on every requested mesh and exterior domain.

0018      action=request.get('action','solve')  #: Preserve the original default reference solve.
0019      if action not in ACTIONS:raise ValueError('This native action requires the Windows desktop build; online preview runs reference solvers')  #: Never fabricate native adviser/CAD/FEM availability.

0036      if action=='convergence':return convergence(project,progress)  #: Execute the original complete reference mesh study.

0041      return solve_project(project,matrix,progress)  #: Execute unchanged reference 2D/3D field formulations.

0070              if 'turn_links' in request:  #: Validate exact connection incidence/path data before replacing an accepted job or field.

0072                  from engine.turn_links import link_plan  #: Run pure source/graph/path validation without loading native CAD into the server.
0073                  link_plan(project,request['turn_ports'],request['turn_links'])  #: Reject stale cuts,wrong electrical owners,branches,cycles and invalid SI routes before starting a new job.
0074              geometry_plan(project, request.get('include_envelopes', True))  #: Keep polygon, complexity and thickness failures actionable and transaction-safe.
0075          with self.lock:
0076              if self.job and self.job['status']=='running':
0077                  raise ValueError('An analysis is already running')
0078              job_id = secrets.token_hex(8)
0079              self.cancel.clear()
0080              self.job = {'id':job_id,'status':'running','action':action,'log':[], 'started':time.time()}
0081          def log(message):
0082              with self.lock:
0083                  self.job['log'].append(str(message))
0084                  self.job['log'] = self.job['log'][-250:]
0085          def worker():
0086              try:
0087                  if action in ['ngsolve', 'openmagnetics', 'geometry_cad', 'design_transformer']:  #: Route transformer design, native FEM and CAD through the existing cancellable isolated process boundary.
0088                      result = run_backend(action, request, log, self.cancel.is_set)  #: Preserve real worker errors and avoid loading two CAD kernels into the GUI server.
0089                  elif action=='mesh':  #: Preserve the original mesh-only branch after the new isolated backend dispatch.
0090                      result = {'project':project,'mesh':mesh_public(build_mesh(project,log,self.cancel.is_set)),'kind':'mesh'}
0091                  elif action=='port_study':  #: Run the explicitly selected reference multiport study as an ordinary cancellable job.
0092                      from engine.port_study import port_study  #: Reuse the shared actual matrix/mesh/domain implementation.
0093                      result=port_study(project,request.get('options'),log,self.cancel.is_set)  #: Retain prior accepted fields when this separate study completes or is cancelled.
0094                  elif action=='convergence':
0095                      result = convergence(project,log,self.cancel.is_set)
0096                  elif action=='sweep':
0097                      result = current_sweep(project,request.get('winding_id'),request.get('values'),log,self.cancel.is_set)
0098                  elif action=='ac':  #: Route a single accepted AC job to the dedicated frequency-domain branch.
0099                      result = solve_ac(project,request.get('options'),log,self.cancel.is_set)  #: Pass the model, run settings, progress callback and cancellation flag into the real complex-field solver.
0100                  elif action=='ac_sweep':  #: Route a bounded AC frequency study separately from static current and mesh studies.
0101                      result = frequency_sweep(project,request.get('frequencies'),request.get('options'),log,self.cancel.is_set)  #: Pass the complete frequency list and common controls to independently recomputed AC solves.
0102                  elif action=='adaptive':
0103                      result = adaptive_solve(project,request.get('options'),bool(request.get('matrix',True)),log,self.cancel.is_set)
0104                  else:
0105                      result = solve_project(project,bool(request.get('matrix',True)),log,self.cancel.is_set)
0106                  if self.cancel.is_set():
0107                      raise Cancelled('Analysis cancelled')
0108                  with self.lock:
0109                      self.result = result
0110                      if 'fields' in result:
0111                          self.field_result = result
0112                      self.result_job = job_id
0113                      self.job['status'] = 'done'
0114                      log('Completed')
0115              except Cancelled as e:
0116                  with self.lock:
0117                      self.job['status'] = 'cancelled'
0118                      self.job['error'] = str(e)
0119              except Exception as e:
0120                  traceback.print_exc()
0121                  with self.lock:
0122                      self.job['status'] = 'error'
0123                      self.job['error'] = str(e)
0124          threading.Thread(target=worker,daemon=True).start()
0125          return job_id

0134          def reply(self,body,kind='application/json',status=200):

0136                  body = json.dumps(body,allow_nan=False)
0137              if isinstance(body,str):
0138                  body = body.encode('utf-8')

0140              self.send_header('Content-Type',kind+'; charset=utf-8')
0141              self.send_header('Content-Length',str(len(body)))
0142              self.send_header('Cache-Control','no-store')
0143              self.send_header('X-Content-Type-Options','nosniff')
0144              self.send_header('X-Frame-Options','DENY')
0145              self.send_header('Content-Security-Policy',"default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' blob: data:; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; object-src 'none'")
0146              self.end_headers()

0003  renderBottom=function(){ // #: Preserve every original matrix/field/study renderer before adding these controls.

0011      box.querySelector('#portCalculate').onclick=async()=>{const serial=++portSerial,frame=r;try{portReport=null;portError='';const value=await api('/api/port-reduction',{circuits:c,input_id:portInput,shorted_ids:portShorts});if(serial!==portSerial||S.result!==frame)return;portReport=value;renderBottom();}catch(error){if(serial!==portSerial||S.result!==frame)return;portError=error.message;renderBottom();}}; // #: Call the shared Python reduction without starting or replacing a field job, rejecting stale replies.

0023      if(s.options.mesh_factors.length>1)el.innerHTML+=MagneticsStudyCharts.chart(s.options.air_factors.map(a=>({name:'Air ×'+a,points:s.rows.filter(r=>r.air_factor===a).map(r=>[r.mesh_factor,r.measurement.input_inductance_H*1e6])})),'Mesh factor','Selected input · µH'); // #: Compare mesh sensitivity only among points at the same outer domain.
0024      if(s.options.air_factors.length>1)el.innerHTML+=MagneticsStudyCharts.chart(s.options.mesh_factors.map(h=>({name:'Mesh ×'+h,points:s.rows.filter(r=>r.mesh_factor===h).map(r=>[r.air_factor,r.measurement.input_inductance_H*1e6])})),'Air factor','Selected input · µH'); // #: Compare exterior-domain sensitivity at the same nominal physical mesh spacing.
0025      el.innerHTML+='<button id="portStudyDownload">Save complete study JSON</button>';$('portStudyDownload').onclick=()=>download(JSON.stringify(s,null,2),safeName()+'_port_study.json'); // #: Export exact original/generated projects, matrices, current responses, stats and defined comparisons.
0026  } // #: End additive study presentation; original field/study controls remain available.

0013  <dialog id="guideDialog"><div class="dialog-head"><h2>Engineering workflow</h2><button data-close="guideDialog">×</button></div><div class="guide"><p class="eyebrow">MAGNETICS STUDIO 0.8 · INDEPENDENT IMPLEMENTATION</p><h3>Guided builder</h3><p>Click Builder and follow Core type → Dimensions → Material → Air gaps → Circuits → Windings → Interleaving → Nameplate/build. The same circuit and winding inputs drive 2D and 3D geometry.</p><h3>Circuits and independent windings</h3><p>A named circuit, such as Phase U, contains its own winding count and winding definitions. Primary and secondary have independent turns, current and phase. Only physical layers of one electrical winding carry the same series current. Reorder those layers per limb to define interleaving. Single, multilayer and interleaved arrangements are supported. Declared totals and odd final layers are checked.</p><p>Signed instantaneous current is the default excitation. Optional sinusoidal snapshot mode uses peak current × cos(snapshot angle + winding phase). It is a prescribed magnetostatic point, not a voltage-driven or AC circuit solve. Phase names do not impose phase angles.</p><h3>Material and image digitiser</h3><p>Select a material, enter linear μr, import numeric B–H data, or click Digitise from graph image in Material. Load a screenshot, enter graph limits/units/scales and calibrate A=(Xmin,Ymin), B=(Xmax,Ymin), C=(Xmin,Ymax). Trace one monotonic positive branch. Scroll zooms; Shift/right-drag pans. Move/delete points or edit their SI values. Invalid points block application; including (0,0) is an explicit assumption. Images stay in the local browser. Perspective photographs are not corrected.</p><p>Nonlinear reversible B–H is available in 2D. 3D remains linear; storing a curve in 3D does not activate nonlinear physics. Temperature/frequency labels select curve data explicitly; no automatic scaling or loss model is implied.</p><h3>2D and 3D windings</h3><p>2D supports full winding-block rectangles or separately meshed round/litz/foil/rectangular turn sections. A region carrying n turns has Jz = ±nI/A. Each winding has both + and − sections, preserving total ampere-turns. Round curves are faceted polygons. Litz is homogenized within each bundle, not resolved strand by strand. Stack depth scales linkage and energy; planar end effects are absent.</p><p>3D uses closed ideal rectangular current sheets at the packing-defined mean radius and axial extent. It computes 3D field end effects in a finite air domain. Winding display envelopes do not represent finite-copper eddy-current physics. Curved toroidal 3D cores and individual 3D wires are not implemented.</p><h3>Mesh, solve and inspect</h3><p>Preview and check fit, then use the model in the workspace, generate mesh and solve. Standard 3D budget is 44k tetrahedra; the explicitly selected extended budget allows 120k and may be slower. The builder shows a mesh estimate. Uncheck Matrix for field-only analysis. Failed convergence is rejected.</p><p>Use 3D field volume for magnitude colours, volume arrows, traced B paths, transparency and capped core surfaces. Rotating field slice remains a single plane. Streamlines are approximate postprocessing of elementwise fields. The solid model view provides part selection, hiding, cutaway and display OBJ export.</p><h3>Results and verification</h3><p>Linear inductance uses independent unit excitations. Native 3D section matrices are grouped by electrical winding, not by circuit container. Per-container winding-pair leakage assumes the selected other winding is shorted and all remaining winding currents are zero/open; the full coupled matrix is retained. Nonlinear points do not have a constant linear inductance matrix.</p><p>Check finer mesh and larger air boundaries. A tiny algebraic residual or energy discrepancy alone is not evidence of engineering accuracy. The closed outer boundary imposes zero normal flux and is not an infinite open boundary. Sharp-corner field peaks can remain mesh dependent.</p><h3>Save and reuse</h3><p>Save project JSON for the workspace, builder input JSON for the recipe, or a digitiser session including the source graph image. Changing raw generated geometry flags the recipe as stale; rebuilding does not infer manual edits. Previous v0.5 recipes migrate with their explicit geometry preserved. CSV, JSON, VTK, PNG and self-contained HTML reports remain available.</p><h3>Release boundaries</h3><p>Independent research software with limited validation. Linear 2D AC/eddy-current analysis is available with explicit solid-conductor current constraints and RMS phasors. No 3D/nonlinear AC, transient, core-loss, hysteresis, thermal, permanent-magnet, force/torque, arbitrary CAD or general 3D adaptive-meshing solver is added. Linear 2D local residual-driven refinement is available from Analysis; its indicator is not a certified error bound. The circuit hierarchy is a modelling definition, not a general electrical network solver.</p></div></dialog>
